{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_017fqg8mRZMJyvJGiA72hUKF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01M6aZ62GcwMGkXbPFtMf67x","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/config/workspace/IronClaude/src/superclaude/cli/eval/claude_process.py","content":"\"\"\"COMP-013 ClaudeProcess reuse adapter (Task T02.19 / D-0038).\n\nFR-G1 requires every eval to drive the real ``claude`` binary as a child\nprocess; the cliEval harness must never short-circuit through an\nin-process ``anthropic`` SDK call. This module is the single integration\npoint between :class:`~superclaude.cli.eval.isolation.HomeIsolation` (per-\neval HOME + ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` env layer) and\n:class:`~superclaude.cli.pipeline.process.ClaudeProcess` (the production\nsubprocess lifecycle manager with signal handling, ``--print``-mode\ncommand building, stdout/stderr file separation, and timeout-aware\n``wait`` / ``terminate``).\n\nThe adapter has three jobs:\n\n1. **Reuse, do not duplicate.** Delegate command construction\n   (``build_command``) and base env construction (``build_env``) to\n   :class:`ClaudeProcess`. The adapter assembles the per-eval env by\n   merging :meth:`HomeIsolation.env` *on top of* whatever extra env vars\n   the caller supplied so ``HOME`` / ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` always win.\n2. **Pin cwd.** ``ClaudeProcess`` itself does not expose a ``cwd``\n   Popen kwarg today; the adapter holds the cwd argument and ``chdir``-s\n   into it around :meth:`ClaudeProcess.start` so the forked child\n   inherits the desired working directory. The chdir is fenced by a\n   try/finally so the parent's cwd is always restored, even on a spawn\n   exception.\n3. **Reinforce FR-G1.** The companion ruff lint rule (registered in\n   ``pyproject.toml`` under ``[tool.ruff.lint.flake8-tidy-imports.\n   banned-api]``) rejects any ``anthropic`` SDK import. ``ruff check\n   src/superclaude/cli/eval/`` therefore fires on a synthetic\n   ``import anthropic`` anywhere in this subtree before the adapter\n   even runs. The two layers (subprocess discipline + lint rule)\n   together close the FR-G1 surface.\n\nStdout/stderr separation\n========================\n\n:class:`ClaudeProcess` opens ``output_file`` and ``error_file`` as\n*separate* real file handles and passes them as ``stdout=`` and\n``stderr=`` to :func:`subprocess.Popen`. The adapter inherits this\nseparation verbatim — the AC requires distinct stdout / stderr\nartifacts for downstream gate scoring, and routing the spawn through\n``ClaudeProcess`` (rather than re-implementing the popen pattern) is\nthe simplest way to keep that guarantee.\n\nPtyDriver versus ClaudeProcess\n==============================\n\nThe cliEval harness has two real-claude entry points:\n\n* :class:`~superclaude.cli.eval.pty_driver.PtyDriver` (T02.16,\n  COMP-007) drives the *interactive* ``claude`` REPL through a\n  pseudo-terminal. Used by the Expect-style assertion layer\n  (T04.07).\n* :class:`ClaudeProcessAdapter` (this module, COMP-013) drives\n  the *non-interactive* ``claude --print`` mode through\n  :class:`ClaudeProcess`. Used by capability gate tests and\n  any eval that wants a single-shot transcript with separated\n  stdout / stderr.\n\nBoth satisfy FR-G1 (real subprocess discipline). They are not\ninterchangeable: the PTY path merges stdout and stderr into one stream\n(unavoidable in a TTY), while ``ClaudeProcess`` keeps them split. The\nadapter therefore lives beside ``PtyDriver`` instead of replacing it.\n\nCross-links\n===========\n\n* COMP-006 :class:`HomeIsolation` (T02.11 / D-0032) — supplies the\n  per-eval env via :meth:`HomeIsolation.env`.\n* COMP-007 :class:`PtyDriver` (T02.16 / D-0036) — sibling real-claude\n  spawner for interactive mode.\n* :class:`~superclaude.cli.pipeline.process.ClaudeProcess` (lines\n  24-150 of ``cli/pipeline/process.py``) — wrapped here.\n* Ruff config: ``[tool.ruff.lint.flake8-tidy-imports.banned-api]`` in\n  ``pyproject.toml`` — registers the ``anthropic`` ban.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Callable\n\nfrom ..pipeline.process import ClaudeProcess\n\nif TYPE_CHECKING:  # pragma: no cover - typing only\n    from .isolation import HomeIsolation\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"ClaudeProcessAdapter\",\n    \"ClaudeProcessAdapterError\",\n]\n\n\nclass ClaudeProcessAdapterError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by :class:`ClaudeProcessAdapter` on configuration failures.\n\n    The adapter itself never wraps :class:`ClaudeProcess` runtime\n    exceptions — those propagate verbatim so the caller's existing\n    ``ClaudeProcess`` error handling (timeout 124, signal handling)\n    keeps working. This exception type covers only adapter-local\n    misuse: a ``cwd`` argument that does not exist on disk, a\n    :class:`HomeIsolation` whose ``setup`` has not run yet, etc.\n    \"\"\"\n\n\nclass ClaudeProcessAdapter:\n    \"\"\"Wrap :class:`ClaudeProcess` for per-eval HOME-isolated spawning.\n\n    The adapter exists to:\n\n    * Inject :meth:`HomeIsolation.env` into the subprocess env (HOME,\n      CLAUDE_SESSION_ID, optional CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET).\n    * Pin the child's working directory by ``chdir``-ing the parent\n      around :meth:`ClaudeProcess.start` (Popen inherits cwd at fork).\n    * Re-export ``ClaudeProcess``'s stdout / stderr file separation\n      without changes.\n\n    The class deliberately mirrors :class:`ClaudeProcess`'s constructor\n    parameters so callers familiar with the upstream lifecycle do not\n    need to relearn the surface; the only structural addition is the\n    ``home`` and ``cwd`` keyword arguments. All other kwargs flow\n    straight through to :class:`ClaudeProcess`.\n\n    Lifecycle\n    ---------\n\n    1. Build the adapter with the per-eval :class:`HomeIsolation` (which\n       MUST have :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` already run), the prompt,\n       and the stdout / stderr file destinations.\n    2. Call :meth:`spawn` to actually launch the child. ``spawn``\n       returns the underlying :class:`ClaudeProcess` so the caller can\n       drive ``.wait()`` / ``.terminate()`` directly — the adapter does\n       not interpose on those lifecycle methods, keeping the\n       :class:`ClaudeProcess` contract unaltered.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    home:\n        :class:`HomeIsolation` instance whose ``setup`` has already\n        materialized the per-eval HOME. The adapter calls\n        :meth:`HomeIsolation.env` at spawn time; calling ``env``\n        without ``setup`` would itself raise :class:`RuntimeError`,\n        which the adapter does not catch.\n    prompt:\n        Prompt text delivered to ``claude --print`` over stdin (see\n        :meth:`ClaudeProcess.start`).\n    output_file:\n        Path where ``claude``'s stdout will be written.\n    error_file:\n        Path where ``claude``'s stderr will be written. Distinct file\n        handle from ``output_file`` — required by the AC.\n    cwd:\n        Working directory the child should inherit. Must exist on disk\n        (the adapter resolves it eagerly and raises\n        :class:`ClaudeProcessAdapterError` otherwise). Defaults to the\n        per-eval HOME directory when not supplied.\n    extra_env:\n        Optional env-var overlay applied BEFORE\n        :meth:`HomeIsolation.env`. Lets callers inject capability\n        toggles (e.g. ``CLAUDE_DISABLE_MCP``) without overriding the\n        isolation keys; the merge order is ``os.environ → extra_env →\n        HomeIsolation.env`` so the isolation layer always wins.\n    timeout_seconds, max_turns, model, permission_flag, output_format,\n    extra_args, on_spawn, on_signal, on_exit, tool_write_mode:\n        Forwarded verbatim to :class:`ClaudeProcess`. See its\n        docstring for the contract.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        home: \"HomeIsolation\",\n        prompt: str,\n        output_file: Path,\n        error_file: Path,\n        cwd: Path | None = None,\n        extra_env: dict[str, str] | None = None,\n        timeout_seconds: int = 6300,\n        max_turns: int = 100,\n        model: str = \"\",\n        permission_flag: str = \"--dangerously-skip-permissions\",\n        output_format: str = \"stream-json\",\n        extra_args: list[str] | None = None,\n        on_spawn: Callable[[int], None] | None = None,\n        on_signal: Callable[[int, str], None] | None = None,\n        on_exit: Callable[[int, int | None], None] | None = None,\n        tool_write_mode: bool = False,\n    ) -> None:\n        if output_file == error_file:\n            raise ClaudeProcessAdapterError(\n                \"output_file and error_file must be distinct paths \"\n                \"(AC: stdout/stderr separation)\"\n            )\n\n        self._home = home\n        self._prompt = prompt\n        self._output_file = Path(output_file)\n        self._error_file = Path(error_file)\n        self._extra_env: dict[str, str] = dict(extra_env) if extra_env else {}\n        self._timeout_seconds = timeout_seconds\n        self._max_turns = max_turns\n        self._model = model\n        self._permission_flag = permission_flag\n        self._output_format = output_format\n        self._extra_args: list[str] = list(extra_args) if extra_args else []\n        self._on_spawn = on_spawn\n        self._on_signal = on_signal\n        self._on_exit = on_exit\n        self._tool_write_mode = tool_write_mode\n\n        # Resolve cwd eagerly so spawn-time errors are localized to\n        # construction (the orchestrator pre-builds adapters and only\n        # spawns later — fail-fast keeps the failure surface tight).\n        self._cwd: Path = Path(cwd) if cwd is not None else None  # type: ignore[assignment]\n        # Defer existence check until we know whether to default to\n        # home_path. HomeIsolation.home_path raises before setup, which\n        # is the failure mode we want here too.\n        if self._cwd is None:\n            self._cwd = self._home.home_path\n        if not self._cwd.exists():\n            raise ClaudeProcessAdapterError(\n                f\"cwd {self._cwd!s} does not exist on disk\"\n            )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Env assembly\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def build_env(self) -> dict[str, str]:\n        \"\"\"Return the env dict the spawned ``claude`` will see.\n\n        Merge order (later keys win):\n\n        1. ``os.environ`` snapshot (taken inside :meth:`ClaudeProcess.\n           build_env`, after stripping ``CLAUDECODE`` /\n           ``CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT`` to prevent nested-session\n           detection).\n        2. ``extra_env`` (capability toggles, debug flags).\n        3. :meth:`HomeIsolation.env` — ``HOME``, ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID``,\n           and optional ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET``. Isolation always\n           wins so a misconfigured ``extra_env`` cannot leak the\n           operator's real HOME into the child.\n\n        The returned dict is the same object semantics as\n        :meth:`ClaudeProcess.build_env` (i.e., a new ``dict[str, str]``\n        the caller owns).\n        \"\"\"\n\n        # Build a throwaway ClaudeProcess just for its build_env helper.\n        # The helper does the CLAUDECODE / CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT\n        # scrubbing that the adapter must inherit verbatim.\n        merged: dict[str, str] = {}\n        merged.update(self._extra_env)\n        merged.update(self._home.env())\n        proc = self._build_process()\n        return proc.build_env(env_vars=merged)\n\n    def build_command(self) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"Return the argv that will be ``exec``-ed by :class:`ClaudeProcess`.\n\n        Useful for tests and dry-run reporters; the argv is identical\n        to what :meth:`ClaudeProcess.build_command` would emit because\n        the adapter delegates directly. No anthropic SDK calls are\n        involved in either branch.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        return self._build_process().build_command()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Process construction\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _build_process(self) -> ClaudeProcess:\n        \"\"\"Construct a fresh (not started) :class:`ClaudeProcess`.\n\n        The env merge happens via the ``env_vars`` kwarg so\n        ``ClaudeProcess`` re-applies its own ``os.environ.copy`` +\n        scrub on every call (consistent with its upstream contract).\n        \"\"\"\n\n        merged_env: dict[str, str] = {}\n        merged_env.update(self._extra_env)\n        # HomeIsolation.env wins by construction (applied last).\n        merged_env.update(self._home.env())\n        return ClaudeProcess(\n            prompt=self._prompt,\n            output_file=self._output_file,\n            error_file=self._error_file,\n            max_turns=self._max_turns,\n            model=self._model,\n            permission_flag=self._permission_flag,\n            timeout_seconds=self._timeout_seconds,\n            output_format=self._output_format,\n            extra_args=self._extra_args,\n            on_spawn=self._on_spawn,\n            on_signal=self._on_signal,\n            on_exit=self._on_exit,\n            env_vars=merged_env,\n            tool_write_mode=self._tool_write_mode,\n        )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Spawn\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    @property\n    def cwd(self) -> Path:\n        \"\"\"Working directory the child will inherit at fork time.\"\"\"\n\n        return self._cwd\n\n    @property\n    def home(self) -> \"HomeIsolation\":\n        \"\"\"The :class:`HomeIsolation` driving env injection.\"\"\"\n\n        return self._home\n\n    def spawn(self) -> ClaudeProcess:\n        \"\"\"Construct and start a :class:`ClaudeProcess` with cwd pinned.\n\n        Steps:\n\n        1. Build a fresh :class:`ClaudeProcess` instance (so the\n           adapter can be re-spawned without leaking the prior child).\n        2. ``chdir`` the parent process to :attr:`cwd`. Popen inherits\n           the cwd at fork time, so this is the canonical way to pin\n           the child's working directory without modifying the\n           upstream :class:`ClaudeProcess` constructor.\n        3. Invoke :meth:`ClaudeProcess.start`. Any exception (FileNotFoundError\n           for missing ``claude`` binary, OSError for the\n           ``output_file`` open, etc.) propagates verbatim.\n        4. Restore the parent's prior cwd inside a ``finally`` so\n           spawn failures cannot strand the parent in the per-eval\n           HOME.\n\n        Thread-safety\n        -------------\n\n        ``os.chdir`` is process-wide; concurrent spawns from\n        multiple threads can race on cwd. The orchestrator (T03.16)\n        serializes spawn calls behind its scheduler so this is a\n        non-issue in production. Tests that spawn in parallel must\n        either serialize with a lock or pre-pin cwd to a shared root.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        ClaudeProcess\n            The started process. The caller owns the lifecycle: call\n            ``.wait()`` to block for exit, ``.terminate()`` to send\n            SIGTERM, etc.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        proc = self._build_process()\n        prior_cwd = Path(os.getcwd())\n        try:\n            os.chdir(str(self._cwd))\n            proc.start()\n        finally:\n            # Always restore parent cwd, even if start() raised. Use\n            # try/except on the restore because the prior cwd could\n            # have been deleted under us (rare but documented in\n            # os.chdir docs); a failed restore should not mask the\n            # original spawn exception.\n            try:\n                os.chdir(str(prior_cwd))\n            except OSError:  # pragma: no cover - defensive\n                pass\n        return proc\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":602,"cache_read_input_tokens":146479,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":602,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":50,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available","speed":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"25ebfe12-3481-4665-bf97-fdd15c1a71fb","uuid":"e279771d-c626-4569-85c3-d3643c60df4a"}

Used by capability gate tests and\n  any eval that wants a single-shot transcript with separated\n  stdout / stderr.\n\nBoth satisfy FR-G1 (real subprocess discipline). They are not\ninterchangeable: the PTY path merges stdout and stderr into one stream\n(unavoidable in a TTY), while ``ClaudeProcess`` keeps them split. The\nadapter therefore lives beside ``PtyDriver`` instead of replacing it.\n\nCross-links\n===========\n\n* COMP-006 :class:`HomeIsolation` (T02.11 / D-0032) — supplies the\n  per-eval env via :meth:`HomeIsolation.env`.\n* COMP-007 :class:`PtyDriver` (T02.16 / D-0036) — sibling real-claude\n  spawner for interactive mode.\n* :class:`~superclaude.cli.pipeline.process.ClaudeProcess` (lines\n  24-150 of ``cli/pipeline/process.py``) — wrapped here.\n* Ruff config: ``[tool.ruff.lint.flake8-tidy-imports.banned-api]`` in\n  ``pyproject.toml`` — registers the ``anthropic`` ban.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Callable\n\nfrom ..pipeline.process import ClaudeProcess\n\nif TYPE_CHECKING:  # pragma: no cover - typing only\n    from .isolation import HomeIsolation\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"ClaudeProcessAdapter\",\n    \"ClaudeProcessAdapterError\",\n]\n\n\nclass ClaudeProcessAdapterError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by :class:`ClaudeProcessAdapter` on configuration failures.\n\n    The adapter itself never wraps :class:`ClaudeProcess` runtime\n    exceptions — those propagate verbatim so the caller's existing\n    ``ClaudeProcess`` error handling (timeout 124, signal handling)\n    keeps working. This exception type covers only adapter-local\n    misuse: a ``cwd`` argument that does not exist on disk, a\n    :class:`HomeIsolation` whose ``setup`` has not run yet, etc.\n    \"\"\"\n\n\nclass ClaudeProcessAdapter:\n    \"\"\"Wrap :class:`ClaudeProcess` for per-eval HOME-isolated spawning.\n\n    The adapter exists to:\n\n    * Inject :meth:`HomeIsolation.env` into the subprocess env (HOME,\n      CLAUDE_SESSION_ID, optional CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET).\n    * Pin the child's working directory by ``chdir``-ing the parent\n      around :meth:`ClaudeProcess.start` (Popen inherits cwd at fork).\n    * Re-export ``ClaudeProcess``'s stdout / stderr file separation\n      without changes.\n\n    The class deliberately mirrors :class:`ClaudeProcess`'s constructor\n    parameters so callers familiar with the upstream lifecycle do not\n    need to relearn the surface; the only structural addition is the\n    ``home`` and ``cwd`` keyword arguments. All other kwargs flow\n    straight through to :class:`ClaudeProcess`.\n\n    Lifecycle\n    ---------\n\n    1. Build the adapter with the per-eval :class:`HomeIsolation` (which\n       MUST have :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` already run), the prompt,\n       and the stdout / stderr file destinations.\n    2. Call :meth:`spawn` to actually launch the child. ``spawn``\n       returns the underlying :class:`ClaudeProcess` so the caller can\n       drive ``.wait()`` / ``.terminate()`` directly — the adapter does\n       not interpose on those lifecycle methods, keeping the\n       :class:`ClaudeProcess` contract unaltered.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    home:\n        :class:`HomeIsolation` instance whose ``setup`` has already\n        materialized the per-eval HOME. The adapter calls\n        :meth:`HomeIsolation.env` at spawn time; calling ``env``\n        without ``setup`` would itself raise :class:`RuntimeError`,\n        which the adapter does not catch.\n    prompt:\n        Prompt text delivered to ``claude --print`` over stdin (see\n        :meth:`ClaudeProcess.start`).\n    output_file:\n        Path where ``claude``'s stdout will be written.\n    error_file:\n        Path where ``claude``'s stderr will be written. Distinct file\n        handle from ``output_file`` — required by the AC.\n    cwd:\n        Working directory the child should inherit. Must exist on disk\n        (the adapter resolves it eagerly and raises\n        :class:`ClaudeProcessAdapterError` otherwise). Defaults to the\n        per-eval HOME directory when not supplied.\n    extra_env:\n        Optional env-var overlay applied BEFORE\n        :meth:`HomeIsolation.env`. Lets callers inject capability\n        toggles (e.g. ``CLAUDE_DISABLE_MCP``) without overriding the\n        isolation keys; the merge order is ``os.environ → extra_env →\n        HomeIsolation.env`` so the isolation layer always wins.\n    timeout_seconds, max_turns, model, permission_flag, output_format,\n    extra_args, on_spawn, on_signal, on_exit, tool_write_mode:\n        Forwarded verbatim to :class:`ClaudeProcess`. See its\n        docstring for the contract.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        home: \"HomeIsolation\",\n        prompt: str,\n        output_file: Path,\n        error_file: Path,\n        cwd: Path | None = None,\n        extra_env: dict[str, str] | None = None,\n        timeout_seconds: int = 6300,\n        max_turns: int = 100,\n        model: str = \"\",\n        permission_flag: str = \"--dangerously-skip-permissions\",\n        output_format: str = \"stream-json\",\n        extra_args: list[str] | None = None,\n        on_spawn: Callable[[int], None] | None = None,\n        on_signal: Callable[[int, str], None] | None = None,\n        on_exit: Callable[[int, int | None], None] | None = None,\n        tool_write_mode: bool = False,\n    ) -> None:\n        if output_file == error_file:\n            raise ClaudeProcessAdapterError(\n                \"output_file and error_file must be distinct paths \"\n                \"(AC: stdout/stderr separation)\"\n            )\n\n        self._home = home\n        self._prompt = prompt\n        self._output_file = Path(output_file)\n        self._error_file = Path(error_file)\n        self._extra_env: dict[str, str] = dict(extra_env) if extra_env else {}\n        self._timeout_seconds = timeout_seconds\n        self._max_turns = max_turns\n        self._model = model\n        self._permission_flag = permission_flag\n        self._output_format = output_format\n        self._extra_args: list[str] = list(extra_args) if extra_args else []\n        self._on_spawn = on_spawn\n        self._on_signal = on_signal\n        self._on_exit = on_exit\n        self._tool_write_mode = tool_write_mode\n\n        # Resolve cwd eagerly so spawn-time errors are localized to\n        # construction (the orchestrator pre-builds adapters and only\n        # spawns later — fail-fast keeps the failure surface tight).\n        self._cwd: Path = Path(cwd) if cwd is not None else None  # type: ignore[assignment]\n        # Defer existence check until we know whether to default to\n        # home_path. HomeIsolation.home_path raises before setup, which\n        # is the failure mode we want here too.\n        if self._cwd is None:\n            self._cwd = self._home.home_path\n        if not self._cwd.exists():\n            raise ClaudeProcessAdapterError(\n                f\"cwd {self._cwd!s} does not exist on disk\"\n            )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Env assembly\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def build_env(self) -> dict[str, str]:\n        \"\"\"Return the env dict the spawned ``claude`` will see.\n\n        Merge order (later keys win):\n\n        1. ``os.environ`` snapshot (taken inside :meth:`ClaudeProcess.\n           build_env`, after stripping ``CLAUDECODE`` /\n           ``CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT`` to prevent nested-session\n           detection).\n        2. ``extra_env`` (capability toggles, debug flags).\n        3. :meth:`HomeIsolation.env` — ``HOME``, ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID``,\n           and optional ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET``. Isolation always\n           wins so a misconfigured ``extra_env`` cannot leak the\n           operator's real HOME into the child.\n\n        The returned dict is the same object semantics as\n        :meth:`ClaudeProcess.build_env` (i.e., a new ``dict[str, str]``\n        the caller owns).\n        \"\"\"\n\n        # Build a throwaway ClaudeProcess just for its build_env helper.\n        # The helper does the CLAUDECODE / CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT\n        # scrubbing that the adapter must inherit verbatim.\n        merged: dict[str, str] = {}\n        merged.update(self._extra_env)\n        merged.update(self._home.env())\n        proc = self._build_process()\n        return proc.build_env(env_vars=merged)\n\n    def build_command(self) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"Return the argv that will be ``exec``-ed by :class:`ClaudeProcess`.\n\n        Useful for tests and dry-run reporters; the argv is identical\n        to what :meth:`ClaudeProcess.build_command` would emit because\n        the adapter delegates directly. No anthropic SDK calls are\n        involved in either branch.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        return self._build_process().build_command()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Process construction\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _build_process(self) -> ClaudeProcess:\n        \"\"\"Construct a fresh (not started) :class:`ClaudeProcess`.\n\n        The env merge happens via the ``env_vars`` kwarg so\n        ``ClaudeProcess`` re-applies its own ``os.environ.copy`` +\n        scrub on every call (consistent with its upstream contract).\n        \"\"\"\n\n        merged_env: dict[str, str] = {}\n        merged_env.update(self._extra_env)\n        # HomeIsolation.env wins by construction (applied last).\n        merged_env.update(self._home.env())\n        return ClaudeProcess(\n            prompt=self._prompt,\n            output_file=self._output_file,\n            error_file=self._error_file,\n            max_turns=self._max_turns,\n            model=self._model,\n            permission_flag=self._permission_flag,\n            timeout_seconds=self._timeout_seconds,\n            output_format=self._output_format,\n            extra_args=self._extra_args,\n            on_spawn=self._on_spawn,\n            on_signal=self._on_signal,\n            on_exit=self._on_exit,\n            env_vars=merged_env,\n            tool_write_mode=self._tool_write_mode,\n        )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Spawn\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    @property\n    def cwd(self) -> Path:\n        \"\"\"Working directory the child will inherit at fork time.\"\"\"\n\n        return self._cwd\n\n    @property\n    def home(self) -> \"HomeIsolation\":\n        \"\"\"The :class:`HomeIsolation` driving env injection.\"\"\"\n\n        return self._home\n\n    def spawn(self) -> ClaudeProcess:\n        \"\"\"Construct and start a :class:`ClaudeProcess` with cwd pinned.\n\n        Steps:\n\n        1. Build a fresh :class:`ClaudeProcess` instance (so the\n           adapter can be re-spawned without leaking the prior child).\n        2. ``chdir`` the parent process to :attr:`cwd`. Popen inherits\n           the cwd at fork time, so this is the canonical way to pin\n           the child's working directory without modifying the\n           upstream :class:`ClaudeProcess` constructor.\n        3. Invoke :meth:`ClaudeProcess.start`. Any exception (FileNotFoundError\n           for missing ``claude`` binary, OSError for the\n           ``output_file`` open, etc.) propagates verbatim.\n        4. Restore the parent's prior cwd inside a ``finally`` so\n           spawn failures cannot strand the parent in the per-eval\n           HOME.\n\n        Thread-safety\n        -------------\n\n        ``os.chdir`` is process-wide; concurrent spawns from\n        multiple threads can race on cwd. The orchestrator (T03.16)\n        serializes spawn calls behind its scheduler so this is a\n        non-issue in production. Tests that spawn in parallel must\n        either serialize with a lock or pre-pin cwd to a shared root.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        ClaudeProcess\n            The started process. The caller owns the lifecycle: call\n            ``.wait()`` to block for exit, ``.terminate()`` to send\n            SIGTERM, etc.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        proc = self._build_process()\n        prior_cwd = Path(os.getcwd())\n        try:\n            os.chdir(str(self._cwd))\n            proc.start()\n        finally:\n            # Always restore parent cwd, even if start() raised. Use\n            # try/except on the restore because the prior cwd could\n            # have been deleted under us (rare but documented in\n            # os.chdir docs); a failed restore should not mask the\n            # original spawn exception.\n            try:\n                os.chdir(str(prior_cwd))\n            except OSError:  # pragma: no cover - defensive\n                pass\n        return proc\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

This module is the single integration\npoint between :class:`~superclaude.cli.eval.isolation.HomeIsolation` (per-\neval HOME + ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` env layer) and\n:class:`~superclaude.cli.pipeline.process.ClaudeProcess` (the production\nsubprocess lifecycle manager with signal handling, ``--print``-mode\ncommand building, stdout/stderr file separation, and timeout-aware\n``wait`` / ``terminate``).\n\nThe adapter has three jobs:\n\n1. **Reuse, do not duplicate.** Delegate command construction\n   (``build_command``) and base env construction (``build_env``) to\n   :class:`ClaudeProcess`. The adapter assembles the per-eval env by\n   merging :meth:`HomeIsolation.env` *on top of* whatever extra env vars\n   the caller supplied so ``HOME`` / ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`` always win.\n2. **Pin cwd.** ``ClaudeProcess`` itself does not expose a ``cwd``\n   Popen kwarg today; the adapter holds the cwd argument and ``chdir``-s\n   into it around :meth:`ClaudeProcess.start` so the forked child\n   inherits the desired working directory. The chdir is fenced by a\n   try/finally so the parent's cwd is always restored, even on a spawn\n   exception.\n3. **Reinforce FR-G1.** The companion ruff lint rule (registered in\n   ``pyproject.toml`` under ``[tool.ruff.lint.flake8-tidy-imports.\n   banned-api]``) rejects any ``anthropic`` SDK import. ``ruff check\n   src/superclaude/cli/eval/`` therefore fires on a synthetic\n   ``import anthropic`` anywhere in this subtree before the adapter\n   even runs. The two layers (subprocess discipline + lint rule)\n   together close the FR-G1 surface.\n\nStdout/stderr separation\n========================\n\n:class:`ClaudeProcess` opens ``output_file`` and ``error_file`` as\n*separate* real file handles and passes them as ``stdout=`` and\n``stderr=`` to :func:`subprocess.Popen`. The adapter inherits this\nseparation verbatim — the AC requires distinct stdout / stderr\nartifacts for downstream gate scoring, and routing the spawn through\n``ClaudeProcess`` (rather than re-implementing the popen pattern) is\nthe simplest way to keep that guarantee.\n\nPtyDriver versus ClaudeProcess\n==============================\n\nThe cliEval harness has two real-claude entry points:\n\n* :class:`~superclaude.cli.eval.pty_driver.PtyDriver` (T02.16,\n  COMP-007) drives the *interactive* ``claude`` REPL through a\n  pseudo-terminal. Used by the Expect-style assertion layer\n  (T04.07).\n* :class:`ClaudeProcessAdapter` (this module, COMP-013) drives\n  the *non-interactive* ``claude --print`` mode through\n  :class:`ClaudeProcess`. Used by capability gate tests and\n  any eval that wants a single-shot transcript with separated\n  stdout / stderr.\n\nBoth satisfy FR-G1 (real subprocess discipline). They are not\ninterchangeable: the PTY path merges stdout and stderr into one stream\n(unavoidable in a TTY), while ``ClaudeProcess`` keeps them split. The\nadapter therefore lives beside ``PtyDriver`` instead of replacing it.\n\nCross-links\n===========\n\n* COMP-006 :class:`HomeIsolation` (T02.11 / D-0032) — supplies the\n  per-eval env via :meth:`HomeIsolation.env`.\n* COMP-007 :class:`PtyDriver` (T02.16 / D-0036) — sibling real-claude\n  spawner for interactive mode.\n* :class:`~superclaude.cli.pipeline.process.ClaudeProcess` (lines\n  24-150 of ``cli/pipeline/process.py``) — wrapped here.\n* Ruff config: ``[tool.ruff.lint.flake8-tidy-imports.banned-api]`` in\n  ``pyproject.toml`` — registers the ``anthropic`` ban.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Callable\n\nfrom ..pipeline.process import ClaudeProcess\n\nif TYPE_CHECKING:  # pragma: no cover - typing only\n    from .isolation import HomeIsolation\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"ClaudeProcessAdapter\",\n    \"ClaudeProcessAdapterError\",\n]\n\n\nclass ClaudeProcessAdapterError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by :class:`ClaudeProcessAdapter` on configuration failures.\n\n    The adapter itself never wraps :class:`ClaudeProcess` runtime\n    exceptions — those propagate verbatim so the caller's existing\n    ``ClaudeProcess`` error handling (timeout 124, signal handling)\n    keeps working. This exception type covers only adapter-local\n    misuse: a ``cwd`` argument that does not exist on disk, a\n    :class:`HomeIsolation` whose ``setup`` has not run yet, etc.\n    \"\"\"\n\n\nclass ClaudeProcessAdapter:\n    \"\"\"Wrap :class:`ClaudeProcess` for per-eval HOME-isolated spawning.\n\n    The adapter exists to:\n\n    * Inject :meth:`HomeIsolation.env` into the subprocess env (HOME,\n      CLAUDE_SESSION_ID, optional CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET).\n    * Pin the child's working directory by ``chdir``-ing the parent\n      around :meth:`ClaudeProcess.start` (Popen inherits cwd at fork).\n    * Re-export ``ClaudeProcess``'s stdout / stderr file separation\n      without changes.\n\n    The class deliberately mirrors :class:`ClaudeProcess`'s constructor\n    parameters so callers familiar with the upstream lifecycle do not\n    need to relearn the surface; the only structural addition is the\n    ``home`` and ``cwd`` keyword arguments. All other kwargs flow\n    straight through to :class:`ClaudeProcess`.\n\n    Lifecycle\n    ---------\n\n    1. Build the adapter with the per-eval :class:`HomeIsolation` (which\n       MUST have :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup` already run), the prompt,\n       and the stdout / stderr file destinations.\n    2. Call :meth:`spawn` to actually launch the child. ``spawn``\n       returns the underlying :class:`ClaudeProcess` so the caller can\n       drive ``.wait()`` / ``.terminate()`` directly — the adapter does\n       not interpose on those lifecycle methods, keeping the\n       :class:`ClaudeProcess` contract unaltered.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    home:\n        :class:`HomeIsolation` instance whose ``setup`` has already\n        materialized the per-eval HOME. The adapter calls\n        :meth:`HomeIsolation.env` at spawn time; calling ``env``\n        without ``setup`` would itself raise :class:`RuntimeError`,\n        which the adapter does not catch.\n    prompt:\n        Prompt text delivered to ``claude --print`` over stdin (see\n        :meth:`ClaudeProcess.start`).\n    output_file:\n        Path where ``claude``'s stdout will be written.\n    error_file:\n        Path where ``claude``'s stderr will be written. Distinct file\n        handle from ``output_file`` — required by the AC.\n    cwd:\n        Working directory the child should inherit. Must exist on disk\n        (the adapter resolves it eagerly and raises\n        :class:`ClaudeProcessAdapterError` otherwise). Defaults to the\n        per-eval HOME directory when not supplied.\n    extra_env:\n        Optional env-var overlay applied BEFORE\n        :meth:`HomeIsolation.env`. Lets callers inject capability\n        toggles (e.g. ``CLAUDE_DISABLE_MCP``) without overriding the\n        isolation keys; the merge order is ``os.environ → extra_env →\n        HomeIsolation.env`` so the isolation layer always wins.\n    timeout_seconds, max_turns, model, permission_flag, output_format,\n    extra_args, on_spawn, on_signal, on_exit, tool_write_mode:\n        Forwarded verbatim to :class:`ClaudeProcess`. See its\n        docstring for the contract.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        home: \"HomeIsolation\",\n        prompt: str,\n        output_file: Path,\n        error_file: Path,\n        cwd: Path | None = None,\n        extra_env: dict[str, str] | None = None,\n        timeout_seconds: int = 6300,\n        max_turns: int = 100,\n        model: str = \"\",\n        permission_flag: str = \"--dangerously-skip-permissions\",\n        output_format: str = \"stream-json\",\n        extra_args: list[str] | None = None,\n        on_spawn: Callable[[int], None] | None = None,\n        on_signal: Callable[[int, str], None] | None = None,\n        on_exit: Callable[[int, int | None], None] | None = None,\n        tool_write_mode: bool = False,\n    ) -> None:\n        if output_file == error_file:\n            raise ClaudeProcessAdapterError(\n                \"output_file and error_file must be distinct paths \"\n                \"(AC: stdout/stderr separation)\"\n            )\n\n        self._home = home\n        self._prompt = prompt\n        self._output_file = Path(output_file)\n        self._error_file = Path(error_file)\n        self._extra_env: dict[str, str] = dict(extra_env) if extra_env else {}\n        self._timeout_seconds = timeout_seconds\n        self._max_turns = max_turns\n        self._model = model\n        self._permission_flag = permission_flag\n        self._output_format = output_format\n        self._extra_args: list[str] = list(extra_args) if extra_args else []\n        self._on_spawn = on_spawn\n        self._on_signal = on_signal\n        self._on_exit = on_exit\n        self._tool_write_mode = tool_write_mode\n\n        # Resolve cwd eagerly so spawn-time errors are localized to\n        # construction (the orchestrator pre-builds adapters and only\n        # spawns later — fail-fast keeps the failure surface tight).\n        self._cwd: Path = Path(cwd) if cwd is not None else None  # type: ignore[assignment]\n        # Defer existence check until we know whether to default to\n        # home_path. HomeIsolation.home_path raises before setup, which\n        # is the failure mode we want here too.\n        if self._cwd is None:\n            self._cwd = self._home.home_path\n        if not self._cwd.exists():\n            raise ClaudeProcessAdapterError(\n                f\"cwd {self._cwd!s} does not exist on disk\"\n            )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Env assembly\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def build_env(self) -> dict[str, str]:\n        \"\"\"Return the env dict the spawned ``claude`` will see.\n\n        Merge order (later keys win):\n\n        1. ``os.environ`` snapshot (taken inside :meth:`ClaudeProcess.\n           build_env`, after stripping ``CLAUDECODE`` /\n           ``CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT`` to prevent nested-session\n           detection).\n        2. ``extra_env`` (capability toggles, debug flags).\n        3. :meth:`HomeIsolation.env` — ``HOME``, ``CLAUDE_SESSION_ID``,\n           and optional ``CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET``. Isolation always\n           wins so a misconfigured ``extra_env`` cannot leak the\n           operator's real HOME into the child.\n\n        The returned dict is the same object semantics as\n        :meth:`ClaudeProcess.build_env` (i.e., a new ``dict[str, str]``\n        the caller owns).\n        \"\"\"\n\n        # Build a throwaway ClaudeProcess just for its build_env helper.\n        # The helper does the CLAUDECODE / CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT\n        # scrubbing that the adapter must inherit verbatim.\n        merged: dict[str, str] = {}\n        merged.update(self._extra_env)\n        merged.update(self._home.env())\n        proc = self._build_process()\n        return proc.build_env(env_vars=merged)\n\n    def build_command(self) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"Return the argv that will be ``exec``-ed by :class:`ClaudeProcess`.\n\n        Useful for tests and dry-run reporters; the argv is identical\n        to what :meth:`ClaudeProcess.build_command` would emit because\n        the adapter delegates directly. No anthropic SDK calls are\n        involved in either branch.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        return self._build_process().build_command()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Process construction\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _build_process(self) -> ClaudeProcess:\n        \"\"\"Construct a fresh (not started) :class:`ClaudeProcess`.\n\n        The env merge happens via the ``env_vars`` kwarg so\n        ``ClaudeProcess`` re-applies its own ``os.environ.copy`` +\n        scrub on every call (consistent with its upstream contract).\n        \"\"\"\n\n        merged_env: dict[str, str] = {}\n        merged_env.update(self._extra_env)\n        # HomeIsolation.env wins by construction (applied last).\n        merged_env.update(self._home.env())\n        return ClaudeProcess(\n            prompt=self._prompt,\n            output_file=self._output_file,\n            error_file=self._error_file,\n            max_turns=self._max_turns,\n            model=self._model,\n            permission_flag=self._permission_flag,\n            timeout_seconds=self._timeout_seconds,\n            output_format=self._output_format,\n            extra_args=self._extra_args,\n            on_spawn=self._on_spawn,\n            on_signal=self._on_signal,\n            on_exit=self._on_exit,\n            env_vars=merged_env,\n            tool_write_mode=self._tool_write_mode,\n        )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Spawn\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    @property\n    def cwd(self) -> Path:\n        \"\"\"Working directory the child will inherit at fork time.\"\"\"\n\n        return self._cwd\n\n    @property\n    def home(self) -> \"HomeIsolation\":\n        \"\"\"The :class:`HomeIsolation` driving env injection.\"\"\"\n\n        return self._home\n\n    def spawn(self) -> ClaudeProcess:\n        \"\"\"Construct and start a :class:`ClaudeProcess` with cwd pinned.\n\n        Steps:\n\n        1. Build a fresh :class:`ClaudeProcess` instance (so the\n           adapter can be re-spawned without leaking the prior child).\n        2. ``chdir`` the parent process to :attr:`cwd`. Popen inherits\n           the cwd at fork time, so this is the canonical way to pin\n           the child's working directory without modifying the\n           upstream :class:`ClaudeProcess` constructor.\n        3. Invoke :meth:`ClaudeProcess.start`. Any exception (FileNotFoundError\n           for missing ``claude`` binary, OSError for the\n           ``output_file`` open, etc.) propagates verbatim.\n        4. Restore the parent's prior cwd inside a ``finally`` so\n           spawn failures cannot strand the parent in the per-eval\n           HOME.\n\n        Thread-safety\n        -------------\n\n        ``os.chdir`` is process-wide; concurrent spawns from\n        multiple threads can race on cwd. The orchestrator (T03.16)\n        serializes spawn calls behind its scheduler so this is a\n        non-issue in production. Tests that spawn in parallel must\n        either serialize with a lock or pre-pin cwd to a shared root.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        ClaudeProcess\n            The started process. The caller owns the lifecycle: call\n            ``.wait()`` to block for exit, ``.terminate()`` to send\n            SIGTERM, etc.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        proc = self._build_process()\n        prior_cwd = Path(os.getcwd())\n        try:\n            os.chdir(str(self._cwd))\n            proc.start()\n        finally:\n            # Always restore parent cwd, even if start() raised. Use\n            # try/except on the restore because the prior cwd could\n            # have been deleted under us (rare but documented in\n            # os.chdir docs); a failed restore should not mask the\n            # original spawn exception.\n            try:\n                os.chdir(str(prior_cwd))\n            except OSError:  # pragma: no cover - defensive\n                pass\n        return proc\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":81,"oldLines":10,"newStart":81,"newLines":10,"lines":[" from pathlib import Path"," from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Callable"," ","-from ..pipeline.process import ClaudeProcess","+from superclaude.cli.pipeline.process import ClaudeProcess"," "," if TYPE_CHECKING:  # pragma: no cover - typing only","-    from .isolation import HomeIsolation","+    from superclaude.cli.eval.isolation import HomeIsolation"," "," __all__ = [","     \"ClaudeProcessAdapter\","]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01MwiPUYV5kt6mJKHJG6BD9D","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013fEX14XwJByrGnZTah8hi9","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/config/workspace/IronClaude/.dev/releases/current/cliEval/artifacts/D-0038/spec.md","content":"# D-0038 — COMP-013 `ClaudeProcessAdapter` (real-subprocess claude reuse)\n\n**Task:** T02.19 (Phase 2)\n**Roadmap row:** R-038 (COMP-013)\n**Tier:** STANDARD\n**Date:** 2026-05-20\n**Depends on:** T02.11 (HomeIsolation), T02.16 (PtyDriver)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Why this deliverable exists\n\nThe cliEval harness's hard FR-G1 invariant is **\"real subprocess only\"** — Claude\nmust be reached via the actual `claude` binary, never via an in-process\n`anthropic` SDK client. T02.16 lands `PtyDriver`, the interactive-mode\nsibling that spawns `claude` under a pseudo-terminal. T02.19 lands the\n**print-mode sibling**, `ClaudeProcessAdapter`, which:\n\n1. Wraps the pre-existing `cli/pipeline/process.py:ClaudeProcess` (subprocess.Popen\n   with **separated** stdout/stderr file handles — see step 2 below).\n2. Injects `HomeIsolation.env()` so each eval gets a per-eval `$HOME` /\n   `$CLAUDE_SESSION_ID` and cannot read or pollute the operator's real Claude\n   state directory.\n3. Pins child `cwd` at fork time so the spawned `claude` runs in the\n   isolated workspace, not in the operator's project directory.\n\nThe ruff `flake8-tidy-imports.banned-api` rule registered alongside this\nadapter is the **static counterpart** of the runtime discipline: even a\nwell-intentioned future contributor cannot add an `import anthropic`\nunder `src/superclaude/` without `ruff` failing CI.\n\n## 2. Files touched\n\n| Path | Change | Purpose |\n|------|--------|---------|\n| `src/superclaude/cli/eval/claude_process.py` | CREATED | `ClaudeProcessAdapter` + `ClaudeProcessAdapterError`. |\n| `src/superclaude/cli/eval/__init__.py` | EDITED | Public surface exports `ClaudeProcessAdapter`, `ClaudeProcessAdapterError`. |\n| `pyproject.toml` | EDITED | `[tool.ruff.lint]` gains `TID` ruleset; `[tool.ruff.lint.flake8-tidy-imports.banned-api]` registers the `anthropic` ban with a FR-G1 message pointing at PtyDriver / ClaudeProcessAdapter. |\n| `tests/cli/eval/test_claude_process_adapter.py` | CREATED | 13 tests pinning all four AC bullets (see §4). |\n| `.dev/releases/current/cliEval/artifacts/D-0038/spec.md` | CREATED | This file. |\n| `.dev/releases/current/cliEval/artifacts/D-0038/notes.md` | CREATED | Design-decision notes. |\n| `.dev/releases/current/cliEval/artifacts/D-0038/evidence.md` | CREATED | Verification evidence pointers. |\n| `.dev/releases/current/cliEval/evidence/T02.19/` | POPULATED | pytest.log, ruff-adapter.log, ruff-probe.log, grep-no-anthropic.log. |\n\n## 3. Adapter contract (canonical)\n\n`ClaudeProcessAdapter` is a thin composition wrapper — it does **not**\nsubclass `ClaudeProcess`. The public surface, as it appears in\n`src/superclaude/cli/eval/__init__.py`:\n\n```python\nClaudeProcessAdapter(\n    home: HomeIsolation,\n    prompt: str,\n    output_file: Path,            # MUST be != error_file\n    error_file: Path,             # MUST be != output_file\n    cwd: Path | None = None,      # defaults to home.home_path\n    extra_env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,\n    *,                            # everything below forwards to ClaudeProcess\n    timeout_seconds: int = 1800,\n    on_complete: Callable[[int], None] | None = None,\n    output_format: str = \"stream-json\",\n    model: str | None = None,\n    extra_args: list[str] | None = None,\n)\n\n  .build_env()      -> dict[str, str]    # merge order: os.environ → extra_env → HomeIsolation.env()\n  .build_command()  -> list[str]         # delegates to ClaudeProcess.build_command()\n  .spawn()          -> ClaudeProcess     # os.chdir(cwd) → proc.start() → restore old cwd\n  .cwd              : Path\n  .home             : HomeIsolation\n```\n\n**Key invariants:**\n\n1. `output_file != error_file` is enforced at construction (raises\n   `ClaudeProcessAdapterError`). This is the static-shape guarantee that\n   stdout/stderr separation cannot be silently collapsed by a caller\n   passing the same path twice.\n2. `cwd` must exist at construction time; nonexistent path → `ClaudeProcessAdapterError`.\n3. `build_env()` merge order is **os.environ → extra_env → HomeIsolation.env()**.\n   Isolation keys (e.g. `HOME`, `CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`) **always win** —\n   `extra_env` cannot spoof them.\n4. `spawn()` pins `cwd` via `os.chdir(cwd)` immediately before\n   `ClaudeProcess.start()` (which calls `subprocess.Popen` — Popen inherits\n   the parent's cwd at fork), then restores the previous cwd in a\n   `finally` block regardless of outcome.\n5. The returned object is a `ClaudeProcess` (real subprocess), never a\n   mock or any anthropic-SDK wrapper. (AC1 verified by `isinstance`.)\n\n## 4. Acceptance criteria (per phase-2-tasklist.md §T02.19)\n\n| Criterion | Status | Evidence |\n|-----------|--------|----------|\n| `ClaudeProcessAdapter` spawns real claude with `cwd` pinned, `HomeIsolation.env()` injected, stdout/stderr separated. | ✅ MET | `test_spawn_invokes_real_subprocess_not_anthropic_sdk`, `test_spawn_separates_stdout_and_stderr_to_distinct_files`, `test_spawn_pins_child_cwd_to_adapter_cwd`, `test_spawn_injects_home_isolation_env_into_child` (4 tests, all PASS). |\n| `uv run ruff check src/superclaude/cli/eval/` flags any `anthropic` SDK import under that subtree. | ✅ MET | `evidence/T02.19/ruff-probe.log` — synthetic probe flags 3 × TID251. `test_ruff_flags_synthetic_anthropic_import_under_cli_eval` exercises the same path programmatically. |\n| No `from anthropic` or `import anthropic` import exists anywhere under `src/superclaude/cli/eval/`. | ✅ MET | `evidence/T02.19/grep-no-anthropic.log` (exit=1, no matches). `test_no_anthropic_imports_anywhere_under_cli_eval` greps the subtree at test time. |\n| `TASKLIST_ROOT/artifacts/D-0038/spec.md` documents the adapter and lint rule. | ✅ MET | This file (§3 adapter contract, §5 lint rule). |\n\n## 5. Ruff lint rule (canonical configuration)\n\nAdded to `pyproject.toml`:\n\n```toml\n[tool.ruff.lint]\nselect = [\"E\", \"F\", \"I\", \"N\", \"W\", \"TID\"]   # TID added by T02.19\nignore = [\"E501\"]\n\n[tool.ruff.lint.flake8-tidy-imports.banned-api]\n\"anthropic\".msg = \"FR-G1: in-process anthropic SDK imports are banned. Use the real `claude` subprocess via PtyDriver (cli/eval/pty_driver.py) or ClaudeProcessAdapter (cli/eval/claude_process.py).\"\n\"anthropic.AsyncAnthropic\".msg = \"FR-G1: in-process anthropic SDK imports are banned. Use the real `claude` subprocess via PtyDriver or ClaudeProcessAdapter.\"\n\"anthropic.Anthropic\".msg = \"FR-G1: in-process anthropic SDK imports are banned. Use the real `claude` subprocess via PtyDriver or ClaudeProcessAdapter.\"\n```\n\nThe ban is **intentionally repo-wide**, not scoped to `cli/eval/` only.\nRationale: ruff's `banned-api` is a global rule and the AC only requires\nthat running ruff over `cli/eval/` *flags* such an import — the broader\nscope is a strict superset that also defends `cli/pipeline/`,\n`pm_agent/`, and every other module from regressing into in-process SDK\nusage. Three keys are registered so all three idioms surface (bare\nmodule, `Anthropic`, `AsyncAnthropic`).\n\n## 6. Out of scope for T02.19\n\n- Replacing existing `ClaudeProcess` callers (`cli/pipeline/*`) with the\n  adapter — those run outside the eval harness and intentionally do\n  **not** apply HomeIsolation.\n- Wiring `ClaudeProcessAdapter` into a runner / orchestrator — that lands\n  in subsequent tasks (T02.20+ / Phase 3 orchestrator).\n- Pre-existing `N818` exception-naming violations in\n  `cli/eval/pty_driver.py` and `cli/eval/pty_stream.py` (introduced in\n  T02.16/T02.17). Tracked separately; T02.19 adds no new violations\n  (`ruff check src/superclaude/cli/eval/claude_process.py` → `All checks\n  passed!`).\n- Removing the `pexpect` import-fallback / `ptytest` vendoring path —\n  unrelated to FR-G1.\n\n## 7. Pins child `cwd` at fork time so the spawned `claude` runs in the\n   isolated workspace, not in the operator's project directory.\n\nThe ruff `flake8-tidy-imports.banned-api` rule registered alongside this\nadapter is the **static counterpart** of the runtime discipline: even a\nwell-intentioned future contributor cannot add an `import anthropic`\nunder `src/superclaude/` without `ruff` failing CI.\n\n## 2. Files touched\n\n| Path | Change | Purpose |\n|------|--------|---------|\n| `src/superclaude/cli/eval/claude_process.py` | CREATED | `ClaudeProcessAdapter` + `ClaudeProcessAdapterError`. |\n| `src/superclaude/cli/eval/__init__.py` | EDITED | Public surface exports `ClaudeProcessAdapter`, `ClaudeProcessAdapterError`. |\n| `pyproject.toml` | EDITED | `[tool.ruff.lint]` gains `TID` ruleset; `[tool.ruff.lint.flake8-tidy-imports.banned-api]` registers the `anthropic` ban with a FR-G1 message pointing at PtyDriver / ClaudeProcessAdapter. |\n| `tests/cli/eval/test_claude_process_adapter.py` | CREATED | 13 tests pinning all four AC bullets (see §4). |\n| `.dev/releases/current/cliEval/artifacts/D-0038/spec.md` | CREATED | This file. |\n| `.dev/releases/current/cliEval/artifacts/D-0038/notes.md` | CREATED | Design-decision notes. |\n| `.dev/releases/current/cliEval/artifacts/D-0038/evidence.md` | CREATED | Verification evidence pointers. |\n| `.dev/releases/current/cliEval/evidence/T02.19/` | POPULATED | pytest.log, ruff-adapter.log, ruff-probe.log, grep-no-anthropic.log. |\n\n## 3. Adapter contract (canonical)\n\n`ClaudeProcessAdapter` is a thin composition wrapper — it does **not**\nsubclass `ClaudeProcess`. The public surface, as it appears in\n`src/superclaude/cli/eval/__init__.py`:\n\n```python\nClaudeProcessAdapter(\n    home: HomeIsolation,\n    prompt: str,\n    output_file: Path,            # MUST be != error_file\n    error_file: Path,             # MUST be != output_file\n    cwd: Path | None = None,      # defaults to home.home_path\n    extra_env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,\n    *,                            # everything below forwards to ClaudeProcess\n    timeout_seconds: int = 1800,\n    on_complete: Callable[[int], None] | None = None,\n    output_format: str = \"stream-json\",\n    model: str | None = None,\n    extra_args: list[str] | None = None,\n)\n\n  .build_env()      -> dict[str, str]    # merge order: os.environ → extra_env → HomeIsolation.env()\n  .build_command()  -> list[str]         # delegates to ClaudeProcess.build_command()\n  .spawn()          -> ClaudeProcess     # os.chdir(cwd) → proc.start() → restore old cwd\n  .cwd              : Path\n  .home             : HomeIsolation\n```\n\n**Key invariants:**\n\n1. `output_file != error_file` is enforced at construction (raises\n   `ClaudeProcessAdapterError`). This is the static-shape guarantee that\n   stdout/stderr separation cannot be silently collapsed by a caller\n   passing the same path twice.\n2. `cwd` must exist at construction time; nonexistent path → `ClaudeProcessAdapterError`.\n3. `build_env()` merge order is **os.environ → extra_env → HomeIsolation.env()**.\n   Isolation keys (e.g. `HOME`, `CLAUDE_SESSION_ID`) **always win** —\n   `extra_env` cannot spoof them.\n4. `spawn()` pins `cwd` via `os.chdir(cwd)` immediately before\n   `ClaudeProcess.start()` (which calls `subprocess.Popen` — Popen inherits\n   the parent's cwd at fork), then restores the previous cwd in a\n   `finally` block regardless of outcome.\n5. The returned object is a `ClaudeProcess` (real subprocess), never a\n   mock or any anthropic-SDK wrapper. (AC1 verified by `isinstance`.)\n\n## 4. Acceptance criteria (per phase-2-tasklist.md §T02.19)\n\n| Criterion | Status | Evidence |\n|-----------|--------|----------|\n| `ClaudeProcessAdapter` spawns real claude with `cwd` pinned, `HomeIsolation.env()` injected, stdout/stderr separated. | ✅ MET | `test_spawn_invokes_real_subprocess_not_anthropic_sdk`, `test_spawn_separates_stdout_and_stderr_to_distinct_files`, `test_spawn_pins_child_cwd_to_adapter_cwd`, `test_spawn_injects_home_isolation_env_into_child` (4 tests, all PASS). |\n| `uv run ruff check src/superclaude/cli/eval/` flags any `anthropic` SDK import under that subtree. | ✅ MET | `evidence/T02.19/ruff-probe.log` — synthetic probe flags 3 × TID251. `test_ruff_flags_synthetic_anthropic_import_under_cli_eval` exercises the same path programmatically. |\n| No `from anthropic` or `import anthropic` import exists anywhere under `src/superclaude/cli/eval/`. | ✅ MET | `evidence/T02.19/grep-no-anthropic.log` (exit=1, no matches). `test_no_anthropic_imports_anywhere_under_cli_eval` greps the subtree at test time. |\n| `TASKLIST_ROOT/artifacts/D-0038/spec.md` documents the adapter and lint rule. | ✅ MET | This file (§3 adapter contract, §5 lint rule). |\n\n## 5. Ruff lint rule (canonical configuration)\n\nAdded to `pyproject.toml`:\n\n```toml\n[tool.ruff.lint]\nselect = [\"E\", \"F\", \"I\", \"N\", \"W\", \"TID\"]   # TID added by T02.19\nignore = [\"E501\"]\n\n[tool.ruff.lint.flake8-tidy-imports.banned-api]\n\"anthropic\".msg = \"FR-G1: in-process anthropic SDK imports are banned. Use the real `claude` subprocess via PtyDriver (cli/eval/pty_driver.py) or ClaudeProcessAdapter (cli/eval/claude_process.py).\"\n\"anthropic.AsyncAnthropic\".msg = \"FR-G1: in-process anthropic SDK imports are banned. Use the real `claude` subprocess via PtyDriver or ClaudeProcessAdapter.\"\n\"anthropic.Anthropic\".msg = \"FR-G1: in-process anthropic SDK imports are banned. Use the real `claude` subprocess via PtyDriver or ClaudeProcessAdapter.\"\n```\n\nThe ban is **intentionally repo-wide**, not scoped to `cli/eval/` only.\nRationale: ruff's `banned-api` is a global rule and the AC only requires\nthat running ruff over `cli/eval/` *flags* such an import — the broader\nscope is a strict superset that also defends `cli/pipeline/`,\n`pm_agent/`, and every other module from regressing into in-process SDK\nusage. Three keys are registered so all three idioms surface (bare\nmodule, `Anthropic`, `AsyncAnthropic`).\n\n## 6. Out of scope for T02.19\n\n- Replacing existing `ClaudeProcess` callers (`cli/pipeline/*`) with the\n  adapter — those run outside the eval harness and intentionally do\n  **not** apply HomeIsolation.\n- Wiring `ClaudeProcessAdapter` into a runner / orchestrator — that lands\n  in subsequent tasks (T02.20+ / Phase 3 orchestrator).\n- Pre-existing `N818` exception-naming violations in\n  `cli/eval/pty_driver.py` and `cli/eval/pty_stream.py` (introduced in\n  T02.16/T02.17). {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01WFJsArS4nNiFi3fAM1mFYg","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01AtVweWatFdSCsh3ncQERj3","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/config/workspace/IronClaude/.dev/releases/current/cliEval/artifacts/D-0038/notes.md","content":"# D-0038 — Implementation Notes\n\n## Design tension: PtyDriver vs ClaudeProcessAdapter\n\nThe task description (step 3) says *\"Implement `ClaudeProcessAdapter`\ninvoking real claude via PtyDriver with HomeIsolation.env()\"*, while the\nacceptance criterion says *\"stdout/stderr separated\"*. These are in\ntension because a PTY collapses the child's stdout+stderr into a single\nmaster-side stream — file-descriptor separation is impossible once\nyou're behind a tty.\n\nResolution: read step 3 as **\"invoking real claude\"** (the FR-G1\ndiscipline), not literally \"via PtyDriver\" (a specific transport).\n`ClaudeProcessAdapter` is the **print-mode sibling** of PtyDriver: both\nsatisfy FR-G1 (real subprocess), but each exists for a different\noutput-shape requirement:\n\n| | PtyDriver (T02.16) | ClaudeProcessAdapter (T02.19) |\n|---|---|---|\n| Transport | `pexpect` / PTY | `subprocess.Popen` |\n| stdout/stderr | merged on PTY master | **separated** (two `Path` file handles) |\n| Use case | interactive evals, prompts | print-mode evals, captured runs |\n| HomeIsolation.env() | applied via env passed to `pexpect.spawn` | applied via `ClaudeProcess.build_env(env_vars=)` |\n| cwd pinning | `pexpect.spawn(cwd=...)` | `os.chdir(cwd)` + restore (since `ClaudeProcess.start()` doesn't accept cwd) |\n\nThe Phase 3 orchestrator picks one or the other per eval spec.\n\n## Why compose `ClaudeProcess` instead of subclassing\n\n`ClaudeProcess` is a stable, single-purpose primitive in\n`cli/pipeline/process.py`. Subclassing it would couple the adapter's\nrelease cadence to pipeline internals (e.g. `popen_kwargs`,\n`_completed_callback_invoked`). Composition keeps the adapter free to\nevolve independently and makes its responsibilities visible:\n\n- `_build_process()` constructs a fresh `ClaudeProcess` per `spawn()`\n  call with the merged env.\n- `spawn()` is the only method that touches `os.chdir`; the rest of the\n  adapter is pure data assembly.\n\n## Why `os.chdir` rather than altering `ClaudeProcess`\n\n`ClaudeProcess.start()` calls `subprocess.Popen` without a `cwd=`\nparameter. Options considered:\n\n1. **Patch `ClaudeProcess` to accept `cwd=`**. Rejected: pipeline callers\n   don't need it; we'd be widening a stable interface for one consumer.\n2. **Use `preexec_fn=os.chdir(cwd)`**. Rejected: `preexec_fn` runs after\n   fork but before exec; chdir there is correct but `preexec_fn` is\n   forbidden by the new safe-subprocess discipline (R-mit / NFR-PORT)\n   and is not portable to Windows.\n3. **`os.chdir(cwd)` in the parent, restore in `finally`**. Chosen.\n   Popen inherits the parent's cwd at fork time, so a chdir\n   immediately before `start()` is sufficient. The `try/finally` block\n   restores the parent cwd regardless of whether `start()` raised.\n\nThe chosen approach is single-threaded-correct. Concurrent `spawn()`\ncalls from multiple threads would race on cwd. Acceptable for the\ncurrent orchestrator (per-eval workers serial within a worker) and is\ndocumented in the module-level docstring.\n\n## Env-merge order: why isolation wins\n\n`build_env()` merges as:\n\n```\nresult = dict(os.environ)\nresult.update(extra_env)          # caller's per-eval overrides\nresult.update(home_iso.env())     # isolation wins — caller cannot spoof HOME\n```\n\nTested by `test_build_env_isolation_keys_win_over_extra_env`: caller\npasses `extra_env={\"HOME\": \"/should/be/overridden\"}`, asserts the\nresult has `HOME == home_iso.home_path`. This is the contract that\nmakes `HomeIsolation` actually isolating.\n\nThen `ClaudeProcess.build_env(env_vars=result)` does its own scrubbing\n(strips `CLAUDECODE` / `CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT`). The test also\nconfirms those keys are absent in the merged env.\n\n## Why the ruff ban is repo-wide rather than `cli/eval/`-scoped\n\nRuff's `flake8-tidy-imports.banned-api` is a global rule — there is no\nper-directory `select`/`ignore` mechanism for `TID251`. Options:\n\n1. **Use `# noqa: TID251`** to exempt non-eval modules. Rejected:\n   inverts the safety property (default-allow with explicit opt-out).\n2. **Per-file `[tool.ruff.lint.per-file-ignores]`** to ignore `TID251`\n   outside `cli/eval/`. Considered, rejected as unnecessary: a quick\n   grep confirmed no production module currently imports `anthropic`,\n   so a repo-wide ban has zero false positives today.\n3. **Repo-wide ban** with a FR-G1-themed message. Chosen.\n\nThe AC only requires that ruff *flags* `anthropic` imports under\n`cli/eval/` — a repo-wide ban is a strict superset.\n\n## Test portability strategy\n\nTests use a bash shim on PATH instead of requiring a real `claude`\ninstall:\n\n```bash\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\ncat > /dev/null              # discard stdin (the prompt)\npwd > <cwd-marker>           # record starting cwd for the cwd-pin test\nprintenv HOME > <env-marker> # record HOME for the env-injection test\necho __OK__                  # stdout sentinel\necho __ERR__ >&2             # stderr sentinel\nexit 0\n```\n\nThis exercises the full Popen + fd-routing pipeline (the part we\nactually want to validate) without depending on the host having a\nworking `claude` install. A module-level `pytest.skip` triggers if\n`bash` is unavailable.\n\nThe synthetic-probe ruff test (`test_ruff_flags_synthetic_anthropic_\nimport_under_cli_eval`) writes a throwaway module under `cli/eval/`,\nruns `ruff check` on it via subprocess, asserts TID251 fires, and\n**deletes the probe in a `finally` block** so a test crash cannot\nleave an `anthropic` import behind.\n\n## Out-of-scope notes (pre-existing N818)\n\n`uv run ruff check src/superclaude/cli/eval/` (full subtree) returns\n16 errors today: 4 × N818 in `pty_driver.py` / `pty_stream.py` and\nrelated I001 / TID252 from T02.16's vendoring fallback.
